12.2 Exercises

12.2.1
Exercise 1 ­ Forward Stop

You will demonstrate your ability to                          Page 12-2
judge the front of your vehicle during
a controlled stop. Drive forward
through the alley and stop with the
front most part of the vehicle (other than unique
vehicle parts at or higher than door mirror height,
such as a bucket truck) within the box at the end of
the alley without going past it. You may stop only
once.

Section 12 ­ Basic Control Skills
Version: March 2025
Commercial Driver's License Manual ­ 2025 CDL Testing System  When you have completed the exercise, set your
                                                              parking brake, shift to neutral/park, and sound your
You will be scored for encroachments and forward              horn. This will inform the examiner you have
tracking clearance (rear tire between the cone and            completed the exercise.
the line).
                                                              Road Test
You are NOT allowed to stop, back-up, or exit the             Remember, the Basic Control Skills test must be
vehicle during this exercise. If you stop, the                passed before you can proceed to the Road Test.
exercise will be ended. Stop your vehicle when you
are parallel to the outer boundary and prior to or at
the cones at the far end of the exercise. Then, set
your parking brake, shift to neutral/park, and sound
your horn. This will inform the examiner you have
completed the exercise. (See Figure 12.3 on page
12-4)

12.2.4
Exercise 4 ­ Reverse Offset Backing

You will demonstrate your ability to offset back and
park a vehicle at the end of an alley. From the
stopped position of the previous exercise and
parallel with the outer boundary, offset back into
the alley, bringing the rear most part of your
vehicle (other than unique vehicle parts at or
higher than door mirror height, such as a bucket
truck) within three (3) feet of the rear of the alley.

Stop with the rear most
part of the vehicle
(other than unique
vehicle parts)
in the three (3) foot box
at the end of the alley.

Your examiner will point out the rear most part of
your vehicle when giving instructions for this
exercise.

If you back beyond the rear boundary of the alley,
you will be required to pull straight forward past the
alley entrance until you are signaled by the
examiner to stop, prior to resuming the exercise.

Your vehicle must be straight within the alley when
completed. Your vehicle must be completely within
the defined exercise boundaries when completed
(other than mirrors or other unique vehicle parts at
or higher than door mirror height, such as a bucket
truck). You may not go beyond the outer boundary
line. (See Figure 12.4 on page 12-4)

You will be scored for pull-ups, encroachments,               Page 12-3
and final position.

You are allowed two (2) free pull-ups and may exit
the vehicle a maximum of two (2) times to check
around the vehicle during this exercise.

discharge. Please refer to section 10 of this manual.              · Do not shift in turns and intersections.

As you approach the student pick up, you must:

     · Decelerate and approach at a slow rate of
          speed while continuing to check traffic.

     · Activate amber warning lights and right
          turn signals.

     · Move as far as possible to the right on the
          traveled portion of the roadway.

     · Recheck traffic.

Section 13 ­ On-road Driving                                  Page 13-3
Version: March 2025
Commercial Driver's License Manual ­ 2025 CDL Testing System  Page 13-4

13.1.13(c) ­ Brake Usage
     · Do not ride or pump brake.
     · Do not brake harshly. Brake smoothly
          using steady pressure.

13.1.14(d) ­ Lane Usage
     · Do not put vehicle over curbs, sidewalks,
          or lane markings.
     · Stop behind stop lines, crosswalks, or stop
          signs.
     · Complete a turn in the proper lane on a
          multiple lane road (vehicle should finish a
          left turn in the lane directly to the right of
          the center line).
     · Finish a right turn in the right-most (curb)
          lane.
     · Move to or remain in right-most lane
          unless lane is blocked.

13.1.15 ­ Steering
     · Do not over or under steer the vehicle.
     · Keep both hands on the steering wheel at
          all times unless shifting. Once you have
          completed shift, return both hands to the
          steering wheel.

13.1.16 ­ Regular Traffic Checks
     · Check traffic regularly.
     · Check mirrors regularly.
     · Check mirrors and traffic before, while in
          and after an intersection.
     · Scan and check traffic in high volume
          areas and areas where pedestrians are
          expected to be present.

13.1.17 ­ Use of Turn Signals
     · Use turn signals properly.
     · Activate turn signals when required.
     · Activate turn signals at appropriate times.
     · Cancel turn signals upon completion of a
          turn or lane change.
